Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The graphs operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 71. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 539 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the graphs space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 71 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 539. QED.
